Stage 2.62 (BUILD 135) — win the fight the moment the enemy dies (a card kill, a Nat 20 sweep, a Bound scan, or a mod trigger), never waiting for End Turn, with enemy HP never displayed below 0.

MIGRATED FIRST: BUILD 134's own CURRENT SUBSTAGE (the remaining Bound pieces write-up, in full) moved to HISTORY.md verbatim before this section was overwritten.

PRE-CHANGE FINDINGS (step 3, before any edit, as the session required):
- CHECK_WIN_LOSS (phase-machine.js's PHASE_ORDER) is only ever the LAST phase label the sequence reaches — runPhase('CHECK_WIN_LOSS') itself does nothing but log '[PHASE] CHECK_WIN_LOSS — awaiting result'. The actual win/loss check is a guard at the very TOP of runPhase(phase), run before any phase-specific body, on every single call to runPhase() regardless of which phase is being entered: `if (gameState.run.status === 'active') { if (enemy.hp <= 0) {...; return;} if (player.hp <= 0) {...; return;} }`. This guard dates to BUILD 053 (win) / BUILD 068 (loss) — well before BUILD 133 — and is what actually ends a fight the instant it's re-entered while status is still 'active' and hp is lethal.
- A card that kills the enemy during CARD_PHASE already ended the fight at once, not waiting for End Turn, since BUILD 053: playCard() (cards-mods.js) calls `runPhase(gameState.turn.phase)` right after `card.effect()` returns, re-entering the guard above with the CURRENT phase — this predates BUILD 133 by 80 builds and was never broken by it.
- What BUILD 133 (the Bound engine) did break: onNatTwenty()'s Nat 20 loop and the new runBoundScan() were both retrofitted onto playSweep() (pipeline.js, new that same build), which paces a multi-face trigger via staggered setTimeout calls rather than firing them all synchronously. Before BUILD 133, onNatTwenty() looped and dispatched every face synchronously, so a kill from it was already caught by the very next (synchronous) runPhase() call exactly like a card kill is. After BUILD 133, a kill from a sweep dispatch happens on a LATER tick, after the synchronous phase transition that would have caught it has already run past with the guard seeing enemy.hp still positive — so the game proceeded into (or past) CARD_PHASE with the enemy already dead in state, and the fight only actually ended once some later real phase transition (ultimately End Turn) re-ran the guard and saw it. Confirmed empirically: prior to this build's fix, forcing a lethal Nat 20 via Dev Tools left the fight screen showing normally, un-won, until a further phase advance occurred.
- Enemy HP display: renderStats() (rendering.js) wrote `gameState.enemy.hp + ' / ' + gameState.enemy.maxHp` straight from state with no floor — overkill damage (dealDamage()/calculateDamage(), pipeline.js) is applied unclamped by design (never capped at 0 in state), so any kill dealing more damage than the enemy's remaining HP left the on-screen number negative for however long it took the win to register.

THE FIX (four call sites, one shared helper): a new checkWinNow() (phase-machine.js, beside runPhase()) — `if (gameState.run.status === 'active' && gameState.enemy.hp <= 0) { runPhase(gameState.turn.phase); }` — is the exact same guard-re-entry pattern the BUILD 053 card-kill fix already used, generalised into one place. playCard()'s own inline copy of that check now calls checkWinNow() instead (no behaviour change, one fewer duplicate). playSweep() (pipeline.js) gained an optional third parameter, onComplete, invoked once every dispatch it queued has actually fired (tracked via a completion counter against faceNumbers.length; fires synchronously if faceNumbers is empty) — never when they're merely scheduled, so a kill from an EARLY step in a sweep does not cut the rest of it short: permanent growth from the sweep's remaining triggers (Zeal/Ordain/Elevation/Cope-style modData writes) still lands before the win is declared, matching the session's explicit requirement. checkWinNow() is passed as that onComplete from three call sites — runBoundScan() (pipeline.js), onNatTwenty() (cards-mods.js), and Novena's own card effect (cards-mods.js), the three places playSweep() is used. The synchronous single-mod-trigger branch inside resolvePlayerRoll() (pipeline.js) deliberately got NO new checkWinNow() call — a kill there is already caught by nextPhase()'s own immediately-following, still-synchronous runPhase(nextIndex) call while run.status is still 'active'; adding an earlier check would flip run.status to 'win' one step sooner and let THAT guard be skipped on its next call (it only runs `if (run.status === 'active')`), leaving turn.phase to fall through into CARD_PHASE unguarded instead of holding wherever the kill happened — a real regression, caught and reverted during this build before it shipped (see BUG FOUND AND FIXED below). "The enemy does not act after it dies" needed no separate code: run.status stops being 'active' the instant any of these paths sets it to 'win', and every subsequent phase transition (including into ENEMY_ROLL_PHASE/ENEMY_ACT_PHASE) is gated on that same guard, so nothing enemy-side runs once it fires.

ENEMY HP DISPLAY: renderStats() (rendering.js) now reads `Math.max(0, gameState.enemy.hp)` for the displayed number only — `gameState.enemy.hp` itself is untouched and still allowed to go negative internally (overkill damage still applies raw, unclamped, exactly as every other build), so nothing that reads the real value (tests, the win guard itself) changed.

BUG FOUND AND FIXED DURING THIS BUILD (before shipping, not after): the first version of this fix added a checkWinNow() call immediately after resolvePlayerRoll()'s synchronous MOD_TRIGGER dispatch, reasoning (wrongly) that every kill path needed an explicit check. Tracing the actual call order (nextPhase() → resolvePlayerRoll() → [my new call] → runBoundScan() → back to nextPhase() → runPhase(nextIndex)) showed this call would set run.status to 'win' BEFORE nextPhase()'s own following runPhase(nextIndex) call — which only checks the win/loss guard `if (run.status === 'active')` — meaning that second call would skip the guard entirely and fall through to unconditionally set turn.phase to the next phase anyway, a state inconsistency (turn.phase advancing past the moment of death) the pre-BUILD-135 code never had for this exact path. Removed before running any test; the two tests below (Nat 20 sweep kill, card kill) both assert turn.phase holds at the phase where the kill happened, which would have failed the moment this call was added back.

TESTS: tests/mods.test.js gained one new test ("Nat 20: a kill from the sweep wins the fight immediately, without End Turn" — forces a lethal Smite-loaded Nat 20 via forcePlayerRoll(), waits for run.status === 'win' rather than for CARD_PHASE, then asserts turn.phase is still ROLL_PHASE) — 39/39 -> 40/40. tests/facts.test.js gained two new tests ("a card that kills the enemy wins the fight immediately, no End Turn needed" — plays a lethal Strike via playCard(), asserts run.status is 'win' and turn.phase is still CARD_PHASE with no nextPhase() call made; "enemy HP on screen never reads below 0" — forces gameState.enemy.hp to -37, calls renderStats() directly, and asserts the DOM text reads "0 / <maxHp>") — 91/91 -> 93/93.

VERIFIED: tests/mods.test.js 39/39 before this build's edits, 40/40 after. tests/facts.test.js 91/91 before, 93/93 after (92/93 immediately after the code+test changes with GAME_CONFIG.BUILD already bumped, the sole remaining failure being KI-18's own build-stamp-vs-CLAUDE.md check — expected until this CONFIRMED WORKING entry was in place; re-ran after recording this section and confirmed 93/93).

SCREENSHOTS (KI-6): `node tests/screenshots.js` regenerated verify/ (five PNGs, 0 console errors, 0 page errors). Diffed by hand against the true pre-135 baseline (preserved in this build's own whole-project backup, since Builds 133 and 134 had both skipped this standing instruction per their own paste-backs, leaving verify/ holding a stale pre-133 set that a plain re-run would otherwise have overwritten with no diff taken) via tests/pngdiff.js's diffPNGs(), 1600x1080 (1,728,000 px) each: map 115px changed (0.007%), fight 6,266px (0.363%), die_action 3,187px (0.184%), card_reward 5,630px (0.326%), dev_drawer 110,631px (6.402%). None of this build's changes touch any rendered layout, color, or text this script's five screens capture (checkWinNow()/playSweep()'s onComplete are logic-only; the enemy-HP clamp only matters once HP is already negative, which none of these five captured screens force) — every non-zero diff here is the same run-to-run randomness (random card/mod offers, random enemy intent, the die's own random Nat/mod layout) already present in prior builds' own screenshot diffs, not a regression. verify_prev/ was restored from that same true pre-135 backup afterward, so the next build's own --compare run has the correct baseline to rotate from.

The Notion "Die — V1" page was not reached this session (no FACTS/GAME_CONFIG value changed, so nothing needed syncing there this time).

Stage 2.63 (BUILD 136) — on-screen text for every checkpoint 3 piece (twenty-one new cards, twelve new mods), the Growth-tag text convention, and New Run working at any time.

MIGRATED FIRST: BUILD 135's own CURRENT SUBSTAGE (the win-the-fight-the-moment-the-enemy-dies write-up, in full) moved to HISTORY.md verbatim before this section was overwritten.

PRE-CHANGE FINDINGS (before any edit, as the session required):
- A card's hand text (e.g. Interdict's "5 block, 10 if enemy intent 12+") comes from CARD_EFFECT_TEXT (rendering.js), a plain object keyed by card id, read by renderCardButtons()'s `effectEl.textContent = CARD_EFFECT_TEXT[cardId] || ''` for every card in gameState.player.hand.
- A card's reward-hover text comes from the exact same CARD_EFFECT_TEXT object, read a second time by renderCardRewardPanel()'s `tip.textContent = CARD_EFFECT_TEXT[cardId] || ''` — same source, two call sites, no separate copy.
- A mod's text comes from MOD_DESCRIPTION (rendering.js), read by faceHoverText() (a loaded face's own die-row hover) and directly by renderDieActionPanel()'s load_pick_mod step (the Load-offer hover) — same object, same no-separate-copy pattern as cards.
- Bound is appended by hand: MOD_DESCRIPTION's own string literal for each permanently-Bound mod (Unison, Accord, Kinship, Concord, Herald) ends in ". Bound" baked into the text itself — it is not computed at render time from the mod's `tags` array (isBoundFace(), pipeline.js, is the runtime check; MOD_DESCRIPTION never reads it). A face that gains Bound only at runtime via grantBoundToFace() (Herald/Canticle) shows no Bound marker in its hover either way — faceHoverText() only ever reads the static MOD_DESCRIPTION string for whichever mod is loaded, so this project's Bound marker means "this specific mod is permanently Bound," not "this specific face is Bound right now."
- All twenty-one checkpoint 3 cards (Tenet, Gradual, Vacancy, Lauds, Chastise, Cloister, Psalm, Reliquary, Vindication, Myrrh, Exequy, Hosanna, Gloria, Oblation, Tabernacle, Jubilee, Threnody, Reverberation, Kyrie, Novena, Canticle) exist in gameState.config.cardPool (added across BUILD 131/132/134) but had no CARD_EFFECT_TEXT entry at all — confirmed by grep, zero hits. All twelve checkpoint 3 mods (Largesse, Tithe, Congregation, Cope, Anathema, Thurible, Magnificat, Unison, Accord, Kinship, Concord, Herald) already had a MOD_DESCRIPTION entry (added in the same builds that added each mod) — none were missing, but Largesse's read "+2 soul this turn, 4 block" though its own effect() (`updatePlayer({ soul: gameState.player.soul + 2 })`) is a permanent gain with no turn-scoped clear anywhere — a real meaning mismatch against the other eleven, which all matched their own effects.
- New Run (#startGameBtn) does nothing while a fight-won panel is open because refreshInspector() (rendering.js) set `startGameBtn.disabled = (dieActionStep !== null || cardRewardStep !== null || riteStep !== null)` — a flat disable, not a silent no-op at the click handler. Traced older than BUILD 129: BUILD 068's own CONFIRMED WORKING entry states "endTurnBtn and #startGameBtn's disabled checks were extended to also read riteStep, matching how they already read dieActionStep/cardRewardStep" — meaning the dieActionStep/cardRewardStep half of this gate predates BUILD 068 itself (back to the button's very first "New Run" relabelling that same build), roughly eighty builds before BUILD 129.

CARD/MOD TEXT: CARD_EFFECT_TEXT (rendering.js) gained all twenty-one new entries, each taken verbatim from the session's own prompt, in the same short-phrase style every existing entry already uses (no card previously in the pool was reworded). MOD_DESCRIPTION's largesse entry changed from "+2 soul this turn, 4 block" to "+2 soul, 4 block" — the only one of the twelve mods whose wording contradicted its own effect; the other eleven were left as worded, since their existing text already matched their effects in meaning (only phrasing differed from the session's prompt in a few places — e.g. "capped" vs "max" — which is not a meaning change). Every mod carrying the 'growth' tag (zeal, ordain, elevation — pre-existing; congregation, cope — checkpoint 3) had ". Growth" appended to its MOD_DESCRIPTION entry, the same suffix convention the five permanently-Bound mods already used for ". Bound" — congregation's own text already named "Growth" in prose ("16 if another loaded mod has Growth"), so its entry now carries both the prose mention and the trailing marker, consistent with every other growth-tagged mod getting the same suffix regardless of whether its own text already mentioned the word.

NEW RUN: rendering.js's flat `startGameBtn.disabled = (...)` line is removed outright (now always `false`). bootstrap.js's #startGameBtn click handler now sets `riteStep = null; dieActionStep = null; cardRewardStep = null;` immediately after logging the click and before flushRunRecord()/startNewRun() — the exact same three-variable reset devJumpToSlot() already applies for the identical reason (BUILD 078's own fix): these are module-level flow variables, not gameState, so nothing else clears them, and refreshInspector()'s render* functions gate purely on them regardless of run.screen/run.status. Without this reset, simply un-disabling the button would have left a stale die-action/card-reward/rite panel rendered and clickable over the freshly-reset run.

TESTS: tests/facts.test.js gained six new tests — every reward-pool card (36) has a CARD_EFFECT_TEXT entry; every offerable mod has a MOD_DESCRIPTION entry; a card placed directly into hand (Gloria) renders its own hand text; a card placed directly into a card-reward offer (Gloria) renders a non-empty reward hover; New Run, clicked via the real #startGameBtn while a die-action panel is open (reached through the real openDieActionScreen() path via devSkipToDieActionBtn), is not disabled beforehand and both dismisses the panel and lands back on the map at the opening slot; the same for a card-reward panel (devSkipToCardRewardBtn) — 93/93 -> 99/99. tests/mods.test.js unchanged (40/40) — no mod effect, only mod/card text and an unrelated UI gate, was touched.

VERIFIED: tests/mods.test.js 40/40 before this build's edits, 40/40 after. tests/facts.test.js 93/93 before, 99/99 after.

INVESTIGATION (before any edit, per the session's own instruction):
- Enemy poison-stack count: GAME_CONFIG.ENEMY_BUFF_POISON_STACKS (config.js), scaled per act by ACT_INTENT_MULTIPLIER and Math.ceil'd inside buildAct() (run-and-map.js), stored on the built slot's own enemy.buffPoisonStacks and copied onto gameState.enemy.buffPoisonStacks by beginFightFromSlot(); applied to the player by enemy_buff_dispatch (cards-mods.js). Recorded by fact F19 (config.js header comment, tests/facts.test.js).
- grantBoundToFace(faceNumber) (pipeline.js) is the one fight-scoped Bound setter — merges { modData: { boundGranted: true } } onto the target face (refusing face 1/20 and a blank face outright); isBoundFace(face) (pipeline.js) is the one shared read, true either for that modData.boundGranted or for a mod carrying the printed 'bound' tag on either slot. clearFightScopedState() (run-and-map.js) strips boundGranted from any face at fight end, leaving the rest of that face's modData untouched.
- Die row rendering is renderDieList() (rendering.js): the weight badge is .die-weight (×N, shown above weight 1), the (single-mod) trigger badge is .die-trigger-count (#N, shown above 0 triggers) — both byte-identical box/font per D-28's no-new-palette rule, both appended to the row's .die-mod-wrap. A two-mod face renders a .die-mod-pair (added BUILD 117, replacing an earlier vertical stack) holding two .die-mod spans with their own inline #N counts; each span's text was the mod's FULL name (modDisplayName()) but CSS (.die-mod-pair .die-mod, index.html) clipped it to a fixed 44px box with text-overflow: ellipsis — that fixed width was measured against BUILD 117's own worst-case pairing and is what produced "Her…"/"San…": the ellipsis itself was eating most of the 44px budget, leaving as few as 2-3 real letters.
- The "outside-roll trigger refused: round trigger cap (10) reached" line is logged once per refusal inside triggerFaceOutsideRoll() (pipeline.js), the shared function Threnody/Reverberation/Magnificat/Novena/the Bound scan all funnel through — nothing previously deduplicated it, so a round that refused the cap many times printed the identical line that many times.

POISON: GAME_CONFIG.ENEMY_BUFF_POISON_STACKS (config.js) lowered from 5 to 3; act scaling (buildAct(), unchanged) now yields 3/4/5 across acts 1/2/3 (was 5/6/8). ENEMY_NAT_ONE_SELF_POISON (the boss's own flat Nat 1 self-poison, a separate knob) is untouched, per instruction. config.js's F19 header comment and tests/facts.test.js's F19/F22/F31 assertions updated to the new numbers; F31 now also asserts all three acts' new values in one place.

BOUND BADGE: renderDieList() (rendering.js) now appends a .die-weight.die-bound-badge span reading "Bound" to any row where isBoundFace(face) is true — same box/font as the weight badge, shown on every die-list container (not gated by the trigger badge's player-die-only check), so a printed-Bound mod's badge shows wherever its name shows, and a granted badge shows on the live player die the instant grantBoundToFace() lands and disappears the instant clearFightScopedState() strips it (both via the existing updateDie() -> refreshInspector() re-render path — no new render call needed).

TWO-MOD NAMES: a new TWO_MOD_NAME_CHARS constant (rendering.js, 6) replaces the CSS ellipsis truncation — each name in a .die-mod-pair now shows modDisplayName(id).slice(0, TWO_MOD_NAME_CHARS) with no ellipsis, and the full name via a native title tooltip on the span itself. CSS's .die-mod-pair .die-mod max-width widened 44px -> 56px (just over the worst 6-letter width measured) and its text-overflow: ellipsis removed (overflow:hidden kept only as a safety net). 6 was chosen after measuring (Playwright, canvas measureText against the row's real computed font) the worst realistic pairing (Congregation + Sanctuary/Magnificat, the pool's two longest names) inside both the roomy main die column and the much narrower #dieActionDieList at the game's default 1600×1080 window — both fit with margin to spare even with both inline trigger badges shown; the 5-letter fallback the prompt allowed for was not needed.

LOG: triggerFaceOutsideRoll() (pipeline.js) now gates the cap-reached log line on a new gameState.turn.roundTriggerCapLogged flag (state.js) — set true the first time it logs, checked before every subsequent log call, cleared at START_OF_TURN (phase-machine.js) alongside every other round-scoped flag. The refusal itself (returning false, not incrementing the count) is unaffected; only the repeated log line is suppressed.

TESTS: tests/facts.test.js gained four new tests — a printed-Bound face (Unison) shows the badge; a granted face shows it and loses it at fight end (via resetFight(), the same real code path the existing mods.test.js grant-clears test uses) while the mod itself survives; a two-mod face's row shows both names' exact truncated prefixes with no ellipsis character and the full names via title; the cap-reached log line appears exactly once in a round that refuses the cap eighteen times — plus F19/F22/F31 rewritten in place for the new poison numbers (no new poison test beyond that, since F31 already covers all three acts) — 99/99 -> 103/103. tests/mods.test.js unchanged (40/40) — no mod effect was touched, only the enemy buff's stack count (asserted via facts.test.js, which already covered it) and pure UI/log changes.

VERIFIED: tests/mods.test.js 40/40 before this session's edits, 40/40 after. tests/facts.test.js 99/99 before, 103/103 after.
